# Airkan Catalog - Copilot Text Version

Source PDF: `Catalog_EN.pdf.pdf`
Pages: 213
Generated conversion: text-only Markdown with images removed.

Image policy:
- Images are not embedded in this Markdown.
- Each removed image appears as a text placeholder like `[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-012-image-01.png]]`.
- The corresponding image file is available in `catalog_images.zip` under the same filename.
- `image_manifest.json` maps page numbers, placeholders, filenames, dimensions, and bounding boxes.

Recommended Copilot use:
- Add the `docs/` Markdown files to your repository.
- Keep `catalog_images.zip` outside the main context unless you need visual references.

## Page 2

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-002-image-01.png]]

Chapter 1: General information

Introduction

Sir, Madame,

Herewith we gladly present you our latest catalogue.
The new prices are valid for deliveries per 01-03-2017.

By means of the main and sub tabs you will find the right articles very quickly.
We have also considerably simplified the hard conversion for inox304, inox316 or seawater-proof aluminium pieces (ALU
ALMG3).

The prices of the tubes are now printed next to them and for the fittings you only have to use 1 single factor per raw
material. Thanks to our price-quality-service, improved production method, investments in new machines, use of
seawater-proof aluminium, .... we are the market leader in Belgium. Our impact on European level is also very large.

Misprints reserved. Drawings and illustrations are not binding.
Airkan reserves the right to change the material, information and prices without prior notice.

Not all materials delivered by Airkan have a zinc quality Z275, this only concerns the specials and assembly materials.
These are: suspension brackets, DGR grills, METU inspection covers and inlay materials.

2/213

## Page 7

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-007-image-01.png]]

Chapter 1: General information
Terms of payment, important statements & copyright

Terms of payment

All our prices are in EURO, excluding VAT. The prices indicated in the catalogue are the correct prices. in case of
discussion (e.g. wrong price on the customer’s order form) Airkan-prices will always be applied.

Important: When the term of payment is exceeded, deliveries will be stopped without warning. The payment due will be
claimed by our credit-insurance company. We reserve the right to change our prices and methods of production without
prior notice. All technical data and information are approximate and are not binding.

Terms and conditions of sale on demand.

Important statements

- If you provide us with your plans, please make sure to indicate all necessary information. All missing, misleading or
wrong information can lead to wrong production. Airkan NV can not be held liable because of these faults.
- Orders can no longer be modified or adjusted once they are included in production.
- In the event of faults, Airkan NV decides on how to settle them. Should you decide yourself, we are not  liable for the
arising costs. if you wish to claim that some adjustments or problems are ascribed to Airkan NV, the latter should always
agree to this and give its official approval in writing BEFOREHAND otherwise  we cannot grant any financial intervention
in this matter.
- Airkan does everything within its power to make sure that your delivery is always on time and correct. Airkan can not be
held liable for any damage, delays, ....
- Any complaints at delivery must be reported within 8 calendar days.
- Pre-assembly of fittings, perforations are not standard procedure but are feasible. Prices on demand.
-The stated steel thicknesses have a tolerance of ±10%, fe. steel thicknesses  0.45 = 0.61 → 0.50
-The confirmation of order is decisive and needs to be checked by the customer. in the absence of remarks, this
confirmation of order will be used for production.
- Our material did not receive after production a pickling process neither a passivation.
- If Airkan is not to blame (for ex. 100% proof of assembly errors), then Airkan will invoice the follow-up of the project at 40
euro / h (invoiced from departure at Airkan Oudenaarde until return at Airkan Oudenaarde including costs such as hotel,
train, etc if this is needed to get to the appointment on time)

Copyright

© Airkan NV 2010

This catalogue and all photographs, drawings, tables of contents, texts, encodings, charts, titles, surveys and layouts used
in it are Airkan’s intellectual property and are protected by the Copyright act of 30.06.1994 and all other conventions and
laws with regard to the protection of the intellectual property. Each use, in whole or in part, without the explicit
permission in writing of Airkan NV is prohibited and will be considered as counterfeiting. All counterfeiting will be
prosecuted before the civil courts and lead to a suspension claim, seizure with regard to counterfeiting and a claim for
substantial damages. Counterfeiting in bad faith is a criminal offence and will give cause to criminal proceedings.

7/213

## Page 10

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-010-image-01.png]]

Chapter 2: Circular materials
Info about the kind of material + standard + informations

Info about the kind of material

GALVA: galvanized sheet iron with DX51D Z275MAC quality - according to EN 10142/10143/10327
Round pressed fitting: dx56 z275 of magnelis z140
Inlay materials: no Z275
ALU ALMG3: seawater-proof aluminium sheet according to 5754H22 quality in accordance with EN573-3/En 485-2/EN
485-4
INOX304L: stainless steel sheet according to EN 1.4301
INOX316L: stainless steel sheet according to EN 1.4404

Standard

Airkan ducts meet the specifications of the tender document 105 from 2014; art. C14 par. 2.2 and Luka standard. Tests of
the density and mechanical resistance have been performed by AIB Vinçotte in accordance with the afore-mentioned
tender document. The reports with regard to these tests can be consulted at our offices. Should you desire ducts according
to a specific standard, please specify this and send us this standard to our offices in Oudenaarde.

Informations

Order model

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-010-image-03.jpg]]

Ø up to 500: 45 mm
Ø up to 900: 70 mm
Ø ≥ 1000: 95 mm

## Page 11

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-011-image-01.png]]

Chapter 2: Circular materials

Calculation of prices

Formulas for the conversion of galva price to the price of the other materials.
Not valid for tube, flexible, accessories and pressed pieces.

Price ALU ALMG3 = price unpressed piece in GALVA x 2,75.
- example: B9 315 ALU ALMG3 = B9 315 unpressed GALVA x 2,75.

Price INOX304 = price unpressed piece in GALVA x 4,49.
- example: B9 315 INOX304 = B9 315 unpressed GALVA x 4,49.

Price INOX316 = price unpressed piece in GALVA x 5,503.
- example: B9 315 INOX316 = B9 315 unpressed GALVA x 5,503.

Calculation of prices of insulated double-walled fittings.
Internal and external galvanized steel.

Always take the price of the external diameter in segmented version and multiply by 3.

Example 1: Double-walled 90° bend 250 - 315
The external diameter here is thus 315.
The price of a segmented 90° bend 315.
Price of a double-walled 90° bend 250 - 315 thus amounts to X euro x 4.

Example 2: Double-walled reducer 250 200 - 315 250
The external diameter here is thus 315 250.
The price of a segmented reducer 315 250 = X euro.
Price of a double-walled reducer 250 200 - 315 250 thus amounts to X euro x 4.

For all other combinations of material with aluminium and stainless steel, the price is available on demand.

11/213

ILMM (accessorie) and ILMV (duct) application:
- for standard duct code S and accessories from Ø 200
- attachment through impression by pliers, pump or by means of teks screws

- zinc < 275 gr.
- stainless steel on demand, other types and pré-assembly on demande.

Remark:
Price calculation with motor: price valve + price motor + € 16,24 assembly.
Price calculation for valve with long axis, motor mount and centering tube: price valve + € 16,24.
Code MDS, MDR, MDSS, MDSV of MDSP
DSV = DS + valve blade with felt.
DSS-P = DS with perforated valve blade.
DSAH, DSSAH, DRAH, DSVAH en DSPAH = 1 side with 12mm border , price calculation : price valve + price AH
DR = lever in PVC only for Ø 100, 125, 160, 200, 250, 315, 355 and 400.

Remark:
Iris duct damper is meant for round ducts and provides an easy way of balancing duct air volumes and thanks to the
reducing orifice arrangement, a uniform air flow is maintained over the cross section of the duct. The Iris is used for
exhaust air as well as for supply air and has a handle to controle the orifice and two tubes for measuring the air flow.
The handle has two screws to lock the damper after balancing. Iris is made of galvanized steel and is covered with a
rubber gasket for airtight mounting. Flow control available on request.

77/213

## Page 80

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-080-image-01.png]]

Chapter 2: Circular materials

Grills

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-080-image-02.png]]

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-080-image-03.png]]

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-080-image-04.png]]

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-080-image-05.png]]

Version

The DGR grills are composed of special square frames, adjusted to the different diameters of the ducts. The louvres are
separately adjustable. Glued airtight foam rubber seam.

Mounting the grills

1. Trace out the opening on the duct by means of the mould : size (A + 15 mm) x 615.
2. Cut out the opening by means of a saw, metal shears etc.
3. Mount DGR with sheet metal screws.

Remark:
Product specifications GE60 - GE60K: Fire resistant ventilation grill for installing in massive walls to prevent fire
propagation. Rectangular look-through grill with horizontally placed lamellas. The GE60K model contains an extra frame
in RAL 9022 (frame in RAL 9016 and RAL 7024 on demand). Each grill is composed of plastic sleeves with intumescent
strips that start to swell at a temperature of 100°C. The fire resistance is 1 hour.
These grills may not be used in combination with mechanical ventilation and are not suited for outdoor use.
Avoid contact with water.
Tested according to EN 1364-1. GE60, GE60K = 2 weeks delivery time.

Remark:
Product specifications GE60XL:
Large fire resistant ventilation grill for installing in regid walls to prevent fire propagation. Rectangular look-through
grill with horizontally placed lamellas with reinforced frame in HDF.
Each grill is composed of plastic sleeves with intumescent strips that start to swell at a temperature of 100°C.
The fire resistance is 1 hour.
These grills may not be used in combination with mechanical ventilation and are not suited for outdoor use.
Avoid contact with water.
Tested according to EN 1364-1.
GE60XL = 2 weeks delivery time.

## Page 92

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-092-image-01.png]]

Chapter 3: Vergaduct materials

Information Vergaduct-system

Vergaduct-System

Principle

- The fitting in our factory, of a double joint onto the push-in part of the male accessories.

- This sealing is made of high-quality rubber (EPDM) and meets the stringent requirements in the field of obsolescence and
stability. It resists a pressure of at least: 300 mm WK (this is pressure of: 3 KPA).

- Small dimensions are able to resist class C, according to EN12237 and dossier ‘105’. The Vergaduct-system, which is
protected by patents and registered models meets the highest requirements of air techniques and includes a wide
spectrum of all possible accessories, which, if combined, guarantee a traditional mounting.

-  Economical aspect: the purchase of vergaduct materials is more expensive than the standard products.

92/213

Remark:
Price calculation with motor: price valve + price motor + € 16,24 assembly.
Price calculation for valve with long axis, motor mount and centering tube: price valve + € 16,24.
Code MDSX, MDRX, MDSSX, MDSVX of MDSPX
DSVX = DSX + valve blade with felt.
DSS-PX = DSX with perforated valve blade.
DSAHX, DSSAHX, DRAHX, DSVAHX en DSPAHX = 1 side with 12mm border , price calculation : price valve + price AHX
DRX = lever in PVC only for Ø 100, 125, 160, 200, 250, 315, 355 and 400.

## Page 136

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-136-image-01.png]]

Chapter 3: Vergaduct materials

Technische fiche

Technische fiche

Toepassing

Regeling van luchtdebiet naar een bepaalde waarde, ongeacht de drukverschillen. Verschillende debieten mogelijk met 1
regelaar
Voor ronde kanalen
In afzuiging of toevoer
Werkingsbereik: 50- 250Pa
Temperatuursbereik: -10°C / +60°C
Bij mechanische ventilatiesystemen en luchtbehandeling

Materiaal:

Behuizing in kunststof (vuurreactieklasse M1)
Membraam in silicone
Afdichting: elastomeer lippen

Afmetingen:

Beschikbaar voor buisdiameters: 80, 100, 125, 160, 200 en 250
Voor Ø125 zijn er 2 types beschikbaar: 1 voor lage debieten en 1 voor hoge debieten

Werking:

Het siliconen regelmembraan bevindt zich in een geijkte, instelbare opening in de kunststofbehuizing.
Handmatig instellen zonder gereedschap van het gewenste debiet door de wig al dan niet te verwijderen en aan de
draairing te draaien
Wordt rechtstreeks in het kanaal of bij een standaard accessoire, aftakking of uitgang geplaatst
Stabilisatieafstand voor het luchtdebiet bij afvoer: 1x Ø tussen de uitgang en de stroomafwaarts geplaatste MOD
Stabilisatieafstand voor het luchtdebiet bij toevoer: 3x Ø tussen de stroomopwaarts geplaatste MOD en de uitgang
Mag zowel horizontaal als verticaal geplaatst worden
Montage volgens aangeduide stroomrichting

Aanduiding op het product:

Luchtstroomrichting
Nominale diameter
Werkdruk
Tabel voor instellen debiet (afhankelijk van de positie van de ring + wig)

## Page 143

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-143-image-01.png]]

Chapter 4: Rectangular materials

Technical information

Materials:
-  GALVA sheet metal - Others: INOX304 - INOX316 - ALU ALMG3 - BACTE

Material thickness:
-  0,75mm to 1,5 mm, depending on the air pressure and the duct dimensions
-  low pressure - 0 - 50 mm. wg. - medium pressure - 51 - 100 mm. wg. - high pressure - 101 - 200 mm. wg.

Duct dimensions:
-  All dimensions possible conform to the Eurovent prescriptions.

Air leakage and construction:
- As per Eurovent prescription class A - B or C
- dossier 105 and LUKA. (certificates of duct from 0,75 to 1,5 mm metal thichness can be consulted at our offices).

Frames and intallations:
-  By means of drive slips (not standard), bolts and nuts, clamps.

Suspension materials:
- Suspension angles -  Mousse or shock absorbing rubbers - galvanized threaded rod with length 1m-2m

Inside insulation:
- Standard thickness: 25 mm, air velocity 12 m/sec., insulation protected by a neoprene impregnated sheet on the
fiberglass.
- All insulated ducts are provided with a shett metal angle in the 4 corners.
- Use of incombustible adhesives.
- Special fixing pins are provided all 500 mm. on ducts with important dimensions or fixations profiles.
- Sound attenuation and insulation specs on demand.

Length of standard ducts:
-  The construction length of standard ducts depends on the type of frame

Adjustable ducts:
-  Some ducts are delivered with a removable frame. This makes it possible to adjust the length of the duct at installation
site. (Those ducts are indicated with the letter "R".)

Tolerance:
-  Accuracy on length of ducts: 5 mm; on dimensions: 2 mm; on α: 3°, on material thickness as per DIN norms.

Attention

If you don’t give information on the airtightness class and pressure, we offer standard class A low pressure.

143/213


---

## Page 144

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-144-image-01.png]]

Chapter 4: Rectangular materials

Technical information(2)

Important remark:
As a deafult the new TDC20 and TDC30 frames are immediately folded to the duct. This improves the airtightness and the
quality of the duct. The loose frame remains E20 and E30 and can be coupled onto the new TDC20 and TDC30 frames. The
standard dimensions are then: cf. table 1.

Attention : The loose frame is not ready for installation. The frame has to be correctly installed and masticated on-site.

Certificates:
Certificates exist on all constructed ducts for quality, air leakage, max. pressure, etc... They are controlled and edited by
an official organisation. All certificates can be consulted in our offices.

Fabrication of the ducts:
Ducts are made from coils of galv. steel with the use of an automatic ductmachine. With this procedure, the greater part of
the ducts are made out of one single sheet, and already equipped with excellent reinforcing. Advantages: high rigidity of
the duct through "Z" reinforcement. Very hightightness, only one seam per duct (from 150 to 1250 mm)(max.
circumference duct 4000mm). Cf. table 2 Pittsburg side seams and Snaplock side seams are according to standardization
the best seams with regard to solidity and airtightness.

Vergacol:
Upon your request we can apply a sealer on the longitudinal seams and on the frames. This is no ordinary sealer, but
Vergacol, an incombustiblen, non hardening saler. (Characteristics and certificates upon request).

Duct side-reinforcing:
In standard production, provided on each duct with metal thickness of 0,75 to 1,5 mm. This applies also for ducts with
negative air pressure, as this kind of reinforcing is independant of the kind of pressure.

144/213

## Page 151

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-151-image-01.png]]

Chapter 4: Rectangular materials

Important remark

Definition of a rectangular duct:
- minimum dimension ≥ 150MM
- contour ≥ 600
- standard length 916, 1166 and 1416 in case of TDC20
- standard length 892, 1142 and 1392 in case of TDC30
- outside standard lengths the price of the fittings will be calculated. For ducts > 0,9 and material <> GALVA the price will
be calculated with standard length 1516.
- in GALVA 0,7 and 0,9

You have to pay an additional cost if you want:
- E30-frame instead of E20-frame = + 2,5% per frame
- A40-frame instead of E20-frame = + 2,5% per frame
- A40-frame instead of E30-frame = + 2,5% per frame
- if default frame is TDC , and E-frame is asked, then + 2,5% per frame
- class C = + 5% with regard to class B
- ducts for high pressure = additional cost according to sheet thickness (Cf. table ‘high pressure‘ pag.155) + 10%

- We recommend to use for Class C: TDC20 with angle in mousse MH20 or TDC30 with angle in mousse MH30.

- Drive slips type SR, are not applicable with TDC20.

151/213

K75
19.86
16

OMSCHRIJVING:
Is elastisch, zelfklevend, koud verwerkbaar, afdichtband op basis van synthetisch rubber.

SAMENSTELLING:
Is opgebouwd uit een dichte hechtlaag met als drager een hoogwaardige polyethyleenfilm.

TOEPASSING:
De tapes, speciaal bestemd voor afdichting van ronde en ovale kanalen, hebben een goede initiële kleefkracht en hechten
zich, afhankelijk van temperatuur en luchtvochtigheid, binnen 24 uur; met name op gegalvaniseerd materiaal of aluminium
ontstaat na aanbrengen een uitstekende en duurzame hechting. Voor een goed resultaat dient de ondergrond goed droog
te zijn; vocht mag niet worden ingesloten tussen tape en ondergrond; enigszins uitrekken van de tape vergemakkelijkt de
applicatie en is tot max. 5% toegestaan; belangrijk is het goed aandrukken van tape tijdens en na het aanbrengen zodat er
op alle plaatsen contact is tussen tape en ondergrond (vooral bij koud weer!). De tape heeft na 24 uur zijn maximale
hechting bereikt. Ondergrond moet schoon en vochtvrij zijn. Verwijder stof, roest of andere losse delen. Breng de tape
direct op de bovenkant van het kanaal aan na verwijdering van de "release liner" en druk deze stevig op het kanaal (pas op
voor insluiten lucht). Tape mag iets worden uitgerekt (max.5%) en dan om het kanaal worden aangebracht. Eindig met
aanbrengen weer aan de bovenkant van het kanaal. Zorg voor een minimum overlap van 100mm. Minimum verwerkings
temperatuur is +5°C. De tape moet droog worden opgeslagen. Aanbevolen opslag temperatuur is tussen +5°C en +35°C.
Deze tape niet toepassen voor buitengebruik.

LEVERING:
Wordt geleverd op rollen van 15 meter lengte en is verkrijgbaar in standaardbreedten: 50mm, 75mm.

TECHNISCHE GEGEVENS:
drager : 0,1mm polyethyleen
dikte kleeflaag : 1mm
kleur : grijs
bonding-time : 24 uur maximaal
waterresistentie : uitstekend, echter droog oppervlak
afschuifweerstand op staal : 9 N/cm (23°C)
densiteit : 1,7 g/cm3 rek : ≥ 700%
vuurbestendigheidsklasse : B2 (DIN 4102, deel 1)
flexibiliteit : uitstekend tot -40°C
temperatuurbestendigheid : -40°C tot + 70°C
vaste stoffen : 100%, geen verdamping
verouderingsbestendigheid : zeer goed
opslag : onbeperkt, zo droog mogelijk

184/213

## Page 186

[[IMAGE_REMOVED: page-186-image-01.png]]

Chapter 5: Accessories

Vergacol

Application:
Glue is put on one of the two parts to be bonded.
The holder has to be dry and oilfree. Both parts are mechanically held together by selfboring ‹‹TEKS›› screws.

Advantages:
- A completely airtight and watertight compound.
- Decrease by 60% of the mechanical fixing.
- Strength increase compared to traditional  systems.
- Huge price-cutting of the assembly of various accessories.

Characteristics:
- bonding, filling, insulating (air-layer), open time - at least 10 min.
- temperature resistance (-40 to +90°C)
- Excellent resistance to UV rays and other weather influences
- Does not contain isocyanates, solvents, acids or halogens

186/213
